FROM (
    SELECT `c`.`branch_id`, `c`.`courier_service_name`, `c`.`city`, `c`.`state`, `c`.`town`, `c`.`address`, `c`.`branch_phone_number`, `c`.`branch_email_id`
    FROM `courier_branch` AS `c`
    WHERE `c`.`branch_id` = @__branchId_0
    LIMIT 1
) AS `c1`
